L6690: main
L6691: </meta>
L6692: <dc:title id="t2">
L6693: First Edition
L6694: </dc:title>
L6695: <meta
L6696: refines="#t2"
L6697: property="title-type">
L6698: edition
L6699: </meta>
L6700: <dc:title id="t3">
L6701: Fowler's
L6702: </dc:title>
L6703: <meta
L6704: refines="#t3"
L6705: property="title-type">
L6706: short
L6707: </meta>
L6708: …
L6709: </metadata>

L6845: D.4 Metadata link vocabulary
L6846: The properties in this vocabulary are usable in the metadata link
L6847: element's rel and properties attributes.
L6848: The prefix URL for referencing these properties is
L6849: http://idpf.org/epub/vocab/package/link/#.
L6850: D.4.1 Link relationships
L6851: The following values can be used in the link element rel attribute to establish the relationship of the resource referenced in
L6852: the href attribute.
L6853: D.4.1.1 alternate
L6854: Name:
L6855: alternate
L6856: Description:
L6857: The alternate keyword is a subset of the HTML
L6858: alternate keyword for links. It differs as follows:
L6859: It MUST NOT be paired with other keywords.
L6860: If an alternate link is included in the package document metadata, it
L6861: identifies an alternate representation of the package document in the format
L6862: specified in the media-type attribute.
L6863: If an alternate link is included in a collection element's
L6864: metadata, it identifies an alternate representation of the collection
L6865: in the format specified in the media-type attribute.
L6866: Reading systems do not have to generate hyperlinks for alternate links.
L6867: Cardinality:
L6868: Zero or more
L6869: Extends:
L6870: Only applies to the EPUB publication or collection. MUST NOT be used when the refines attribute is present.
L6871: Example:
L6872: <link rel="alternate" href="package.json"
L6873: media-type="application/json-ld"/>
L6874: D.4.1.2 marc21xml-record (deprecated)
L6875: Use of the marc21xml-record keyword is deprecated.
L6876: It is replaced by the record keyword with the media-type attribute value
L6877: "application/marcxml+xml".
L6878: Refer to the marc21xml-record
L6879: property definition in [epubpublications-30] for more information.
L6880: D.4.1.3 mods-record (deprecated)
L6881: Use of the mods-record keyword is deprecated. It
L6882: is replaced by the record keyword with the media-type attribute value
L6883: "application/mods+xml".
L6884: Refer to the mods-record
L6885: property definition in [epubpublications-30] for more information.
L6886: D.4.1.4 onix-record (deprecated)
L6887: Use of the onix-record keyword is deprecated. It
L6888: is replaced by the record keyword with the properties attribute value onix.
L6889: Refer to the onix-record
L6890: property definition in [epubpublications-30] for more information.
L6891: D.4.1.5 record
L6892: Name:
L6893: record
L6894: Description:
L6895: Indicates that the referenced resource is a metadata record.
L6896: The media type of the record MUST be identified in the media-type attribute when
L6897: this keyword is specified.
L6898: For a list of commonly linked metadata record types, refer to the EPUB Linked Metadata
L6899: Guide
L6900: If the type of record cannot be identified from the media type, an identifier property can be assigned in the
L6901: properties attribute.
L6902: Cardinality:
L6903: Zero or more
L6904: Extends:
L6905: Only applies to the EPUB publication or collection. MUST NOT be used when the refines attribute is present.
L6906: Example:
L6907: <link rel="record" href="book/52.atom"
L6908: media-type="application/atom+xml;type=entry;profile=opds-catalog"/>
L6909: D.4.1.6 voicing
L6910: Name:
L6911: voicing
L6912: Description:
L6913: Indicates that the referenced audio file provides an aural representation of the
L6914: expression or resource (typically, the title or creator) specified by the
L6915: refines attribute.
L6916: The media type of the audio file MUST be identified in the media-type attribute when
L6917: this keyword is specified.
L6918: Cardinality:
L6919: Zero or more
L6920: Extends:
L6921: All properties. The refines attribute
L6922: MUST be present when this value is used.
L6923: Example:
L6924: <link refines="#title" rel="voicing" media-type="audio/mpeg"
L6925: href="title.mp3" />
L6926: D.4.1.7 xml-signature (deprecated)
L6927: Use of the xml-signature keyword is deprecated.
L6928: It is not replaced by another linking method.
L6929: Refer to the xml-signature
L6930: property definition in [epubpublications-30] for more information.
L6931: D.4.1.8 xmp-record (deprecated)
L6932: Use of the xmp-record keyword is deprecated.
L6933: Refer to the xmp-record
L6934: property definition in [epubpublications-30] for more information.
L6935: D.4.2 Link properties
L6936: The following values can be used in the link element's
L6937: properties attribute to establish the type of record a referenced resource represents.
L6938: These values are provided for record formats that cannot be uniquely identified by their media
L6939: type.
L6940: D.4.2.1 onix
L6941: Name:
L6942: onix
L6943: Description:
L6944: The onix property indicates the referenced resource is an ONIX record
L6945: [onix].
L6946: Example:
L6947: <link rel="record" href="pub/meta/nor-wood-onix.xml"
L6948: media-type="application/xml" properties="onix"/>
L6949: D.5 Package rendering vocabulary
L6950: The prefix URL for referencing these properties is
L6951: http://www.idpf.org/vocab/rendition/#.
L6952: The "rendition:" prefix is reserved for
L6953: use with the package rendering properties and does not have to be declared in the
L6954: package document.
L6955: Note
L6956: Unlike the other vocabularies in this appendix, the properties in the Package Rendering Vocabulary
L6957: consist of a mix of properties (expressed in meta elements)
L6958: and spine overrides (expressed on itemref elements).
L6959: The usage requirements are also defined in 8. Layout rendering control not in this appendix.
L6960: The following table provides a map to the properties, overrides, and where they are defined.
L6961: Property
L6962: Overrides
L6963: Defined in
L6964: rendition:layout
L6965: rendition:layout-pre-paginated
L6966: rendition:layout-reflowable
L6967: 8.2.2.1 Layout
L6968: rendition:orientation
L6969: rendition:orientation-auto
L6970: rendition:orientation-landscape
L6971: rendition:orientation-portrait
L6972: 8.2.2.2 Orientation
L6973: rendition:spread
L6974: rendition:spread-auto
L6975: rendition:spread-both
L6976: rendition:spread-landscape
L6977: rendition:spread-none
L6978: rendition:spread-portrait (Deprecated)
L6979: 8.2.2.3 Synthetic spreads
L6980: —
L6981: rendition:page-spread-center
L6982: rendition:page-spread-left
L6983: rendition:page-spread-right
L6984: 8.2.2.4 Spread placement
L6985: rendition:viewport (Deprecated)
L6986: —
L6987: 8.2.2.5 Viewport dimensions (deprecated)
L6988: rendition:flow
L6989: rendition:flow-paginated
L6990: rendition:flow-scrolled-continuous
L6991: rendition:flow-scrolled-doc
L6992: rendition:flow-auto
L6993: 8.3.1 The rendition:flow property
L6994: —
L6995: rendition:align-x-center
L6996: 8.3.2 The rendition:align-x-center property
L6997: D.5.1 Custom rendering properties
L6998: Reading system developers may introduce functionality not defined in this specification to address
L6999: reading system-specific issues rendering EPUB content documents.
L7000: To facilitate this experimentation, EPUB creators MAY include custom properties and spine
L7001: overrides for use in the package document provided they do not use the rendition:
L7002: prefix.
L7003: Note
L7004: Custom properties should only address rendering issues specific to a particular reading system. This
L7005: specification should be extended to provide extensions that multiple independent reading systems can
L7006: use.
L7007: D.6 Manifest properties vocabulary
L7008: The properties in this vocabulary are usable in the manifest item
L7009: element's properties attribute.
L7010: The prefix URL for referencing these properties is
L7011: http://idpf.org/epub/vocab/package/item/#.
L7012: D.6.1 cover-image
L7013: Name:
L7014: cover-image
L7015: Description:
L7016: The cover-image property identifies the described publication resource as
L7017: the cover image for the EPUB publication.
L7018: Applies to:
L7019: All raster and vector image types
L7020: Cardinality:
L7021: Zero or one
L7022: D.6.2 mathml
L7023: Name:
L7024: mathml
L7025: Description:
L7026: The mathml property indicates that the described publication resource
L7027: contains one or more instances of MathML markup.
L7028: Applies to:
L7029: EPUB content documents
L7030: Cardinality:
L7031: Zero or more
L7032: D.6.3 nav
L7033: Name:
L7034: nav
L7035: Description:
L7036: The nav property indicates that the described publication resource
L7037: constitutes the EPUB navigation document of the EPUB publication.
L7038: Applies to:
L7039: The EPUB navigation document
L7040: Cardinality:
L7041: Exactly one
L7042: D.6.4 remote-resources
L7043: Name:
L7044: remote-resources
L7045: Description:
L7046: The remote-resources property indicates that the described publication resource contains one or more internal references to other publication resources
L7047: that are located outside of the EPUB container.
L7048: Refer to 3.6 Resource locations for more information.
L7049: Applies to:
L7050: All publication resources with the capability of internal referencing
L7051: (e.g., XHTML content documents, SVG content documents,
L7052: CSS style sheets and media overlay documents).
L7053: Cardinality:
L7054: Zero or more
L7055: D.6.5 scripted
L7056: Name:
L7057: scripted
L7058: Description:
L7059: The scripted property indicates that the described publication resource is
L7060: a scripted content document (i.e., contains scripting and/or [html] form
L7061: elements).
L7062: Applies to:
L7063: EPUB content documents
L7064: Cardinality:
L7065: Zero or more
L7066: D.6.6 svg
L7067: Name:
L7068: svg
L7069: Description:
L7070: The svg property indicates that the described publication resource
L7071: embeds one or more instances of SVG markup.
L7072: This property MUST be set when SVG markup is included directly in the resource and
L7073: MAY be set when the SVG is referenced from the resource (e.g., from an [html]
L7074: img, object or iframe element).
L7075: Applies to:
L7076: XHTML content documents; the value is implied for SVG content documents.
L7077: Cardinality:
L7078: Zero or more
L7079: D.6.7 switch
L7080: Name:
L7081: switch
L7082: Description:
L7083: The switch property indicates that the described publication resource
L7084: contains one or more instances of the deprecated epub:switch element.
L7085: Applies to:
L7086: XHTML content documents.
L7087: Cardinality:
L7088: Zero or more
L7089: D.7 Spine properties vocabulary
L7090: The properties in this vocabulary are usable in the spine itemref element's
L7091: properties attribute.
L7092: The prefix URL for referencing these properties is
L7093: http://idpf.org/epub/vocab/package/itemref/#.
L7094: D.7.1 page-spread-left
L7095: Name:
L7096: page-spread-left
L7097: Description:
L7098: The page-spread-left property indicates that the first page of the
L7099: associated item element's EPUB content document represents the
L7100: left-hand side of a two-page spread.
L7101: The rendition:page-spread-left
L7102: property is an alias for this property. Refer to 8.2.2.4 Spread placement
L7103: for more information about their use.
L7104: D.7.2 page-spread-right
L7105: Name:
L7106: page-spread-right
L7107: Description:
L7108: The page-spread-right property indicates that the first page of the
L7109: associated item element's EPUB content document
L7110: represents the right-hand side of a two-page spread.
L7111: The rendition:page-spread-right
L7112: property is an alias for this property. Refer to 8.2.2.4 Spread placement
L7113: for more information about their use.
L7114: D.7.3 Examples
L7115: Example 98: Identifying a two-page spread in the spine
L7116: <spine>
L7117: <itemref
L7118: idref="title"/>
L7119: <itemref
L7120: idref="ps-1-l"
L7121: properties="page-spread-left"/>
L7122: <itemref
L7123: idref="ps-1-r"
L7124: properties="page-spread-right"/>
L7125: <itemref
L7126: idref="toc"/>
L7127: …
L7128: </spine>
L7129: D.8 Media overlays vocabulary
L7130: The properties in this vocabulary are usable in the meta element's
L7131: property attribute.
L7132: The prefix URL for referencing these properties is
L7133: http://www.idpf.org/epub/vocab/overlays/#.
L7134: The prefix "media:" is reserved for use with
L7135: properties in this vocabulary and does not have to be declared in the package document.
L7136: D.8.1 active-class
L7137: Name:
L7138: active-class
L7139: Description:
L7140: EPUB creator-defined CSS class name to apply to the currently playing EPUB content document
L7141: element.
L7142: Allowed value(s):
L7143: xsd:string
L7144: Cardinality:
L7145: Zero or one
L7146: Example:
L7147: <meta
L7148: property="media:active-class">-epub-media-overlay-active</meta>
L7149: D.8.2 duration
L7150: Name:
L7151: duration
L7152: Description:
L7153: The duration of the entire presentation or of a specific media overlay document.
L7154: The specified durations account for the audio clips known at authoring time, and so
L7155: exclude live streaming from external resources and speech synthesis.
L7156: Allowed value(s):
L7157: MUST be a [smil3] clock value.
L7158: Cardinality:
L7159: Exactly one for the EPUB publication and for each media overlay
L7160: document.
L7161: Example:
L7162: <meta property="media:duration">1:36:20</meta>
L7163: D.8.3 narrator
L7164: Name:
L7165: narrator
L7166: Description:
L7167: Name of the narrator.
L7168: Allowed value(s):
L7169: xsd:string
L7170: Cardinality:
L7171: Zero or more
L7172: Example:
L7173: <meta property="media:narrator">Joe Speaker</meta>
L7174: D.8.4 playback-active-class
L7175: Name:
L7176: playback-active-class
L7177: Description:
L7178: Author-defined CSS class name to apply to the EPUB content document's
L7179: document element when playback is active.
L7180: Allowed value(s):
L7181: xsd:string
L7182: Cardinality:
L7183: Zero or one
L7184: Example:
L7185: <meta
L7186: property="media:playback-active-class">-epub-media-overlay-playing</meta>
L7187: E. Prefixed CSS properties
L7188: This appendix describes the prefixed CSS properties supported by EPUB.
L7189: Note
L7190: The prefix definitions are no longer being synchronized with their CSS counterparts. In some
L7191: cases, the unprefixed versions of these properties now support additional values. Reading systems
L7192: may not support the new syntax with the prefixed properties, so EPUB creators are advised to use the
L7193: unprefixed versions for newer features.
L7194: E.1 CSS writing modes
L7195: This section describes the -epub- prefixed properties for [css-writing-modes-3].
L7196: E.1.1 The -epub-text-orientation property
L7197: This property is a prefixed version of the text-orientation
L7198: property [css-writing-modes-3].
L7199: Name:
L7200: -epub-text-orientation
L7201: Value:
L7202: mixed | upright | sideways | sideways-right
L7203: For compatibility with existing content, the -epub-text-orientation property also
L7204: supports the deprecated vertical-right, rotate-right, and
L7205: rotate-normal keywords. The following table specifies the effect these have
L7206: when specified.
L7207: Deprecated value
L7208: Value to be used
L7209: vertical-right
L7210: mixed
L7211: rotate-right
L7212: sideways
L7213: rotate-normal
L7214: sideways
L7215: E.1.2 The -epub-writing-mode property
L7216: This property is a prefixed version of the writing-mode property
L7217: [css-writing-modes-3], with the same syntax and behavior.
L7218: Name:
L7219: -epub-writing-mode
L7220: Value:
L7221: horizontal-tb | vertical-rl | vertical-lr
L7222: E.1.3 The -epub-text-combine-horizontal and -epub-text-combine
L7223: properties
L7224: These properties are prefixed versions of the text-combine-upright
L7225: property [css-writing-modes-3], although -epub-text-combine is
L7226: deprecated.
L7227: Name:
L7228: -epub-text-combine-horizontal
L7229: Value:
L7230: none | all
L7231: Name:
L7232: -epub-text-combine (deprecated)
L7233: Value:
L7234: none | horizontal | horizontal <number>
L7235: For compatibility with existing content, the -epub-text-combine-horizontal and
L7236: -epub-text-combine properties also support a number of deprecated keywords. The
L7237: following table specifies the effect these have when specified.
L7238: Prefixed version
L7239: CSS equivalent
L7240: -epub-text-combine-horizontal: none
L7241: text-combine-upright: none
L7242: -epub-text-combine-horizontal: all
L7243: text-combine-upright: all
L7244: -epub-text-combine: none
L7245: text-combine-upright: none
L7246: -epub-text-combine: horizontal
L7247: text-combine-upright: all
L7248: -epub-text-combine: horizontal <number>
L7249: no equivalent
L7250: E.2 CSS text level 3
L7251: This section describes the -epub- prefixed properties (and one prefixed value) for
L7252: [css-text-3].
L7253: E.2.1 The -epub-hyphens property
L7254: This property is a prefixed version of the hyphens property [css-text-3].
L7255: Name:
L7256: -epub-hyphens
L7257: Value:
L7258: none | manual | auto | all
L7259: For compatibility with existing content, the -epub-hyphens property also supports
L7260: the deprecated all keyword. The value is no longer supported in CSS and there is no
L7261: equivalent to use in its place.
L7262: E.2.2 The -epub-line-break property
L7263: This property is a prefixed version of the line-break property [css-text-3].
L7264: Name:
L7265: -epub-line-break
L7266: Value:
L7267: auto | loose | normal | strict
L7268: E.2.3 The -epub-text-align-last property
L7269: This property is a prefixed version of the text-align-last property [css-text-3].
L7270: Name:
L7271: -epub-text-align-last
L7272: Value:
L7273: auto | start | end | left | right | center | justify
L7274: E.2.4 The -epub-word-break property
L7275: This property is a prefixed version of the word-break property [css-text-3].
L7276: Name:
L7277: -epub-word-break
L7278: Value:
L7279: normal | keep-all | break-all
L7280: E.2.5 The text-transform property
L7281: This property is a prefixed value for the text-transform property [css-text-3].
L7282: Name:
L7283: text-transform
L7284: Value:
L7285: -epub-fullwidth
L7286: For compatibility with existing content, the text-transform property also supports
L7287: the deprecated -epub-fullwidth keyword. When specified, this has the same effect as
L7288: text-transform: full-width.
L7289: E.3 CSS text decoration level 3
L7290: This section describes the -epub- prefixed properties for [css-text-decor-3].
L7291: E.3.1 The -epub-text-emphasis-color Property
L7292: This property is a prefixed version of the text-emphasis-color
L7293: property [css-text-decor-3].
L7294: Name:
L7295: -epub-text-emphasis-color
L7296: Value:
L7297: <color>
L7298: E.3.2 The -epub-text-emphasis-position property
L7299: This property is a prefixed version of the text-emphasis-position property [css-text-decor-3].
L7300: Name:
L7301: -epub-text-emphasis-position
L7302: Value:
L7303: [ over | under ] && [ right | left ]
L7304: E.3.3 The -epub-text-emphasis-style property
L7305: This property is a prefixed version of the text-emphasis-style
L7306: property [css-text-decor-3].
L7307: Name:
L7308: -epub-text-emphasis-style
L7309: Value:
L7310: none | [ [ filled | open ] || [ dot | circle | double-circle | triangle | sesame ]
L7311: ] | <string>
L7312: E.3.4 The -epub-text-underline-position property
L7313: This property is a prefixed version of the text-underline-position property [css-text-decor-3].
L7314: Name:
L7315: -epub-text-underline-position
L7316: Value:
L7317: auto | [ under || [ left | right ] ] | alphabetic
L7318: For compatibility with existing content, the value -epub-text-underline-position
L7319: property also supports the deprecated alphabetic keyword. When specified, this has
L7320: the same effect as text-underline-position: auto.
L7321: F. The viewport meta tag
L7322: F.1 Introduction
L7323: This section is non-normative.
L7324: As the Safari HTML definition of the viewport meta tag, that was used in earlier
L7325: versions of EPUB 3, is not an officially recognized standard, this specification defines a
L7326: basic syntax in order to allow EPUB creators to express width and
L7327: height dimensions for use rendering fixed-layout documents.
L7328: The syntax of this grammar is also influenced by the parsing algorithm for the viewport
L7329: meta tag, as defined in [css-viewport-1].
L7330: The syntax is intentionally left as generic as possible as it is not in this specification's scope to
L7331: define all the possible properties and values. It only defines the basic requirements for defining a
L7332: property and value pair as well as the possible separators between expressions.
L7333: F.2 Syntax
L7334: For fixed-layout documents, a viewport
L7335: meta tag [html] MUST have name
L7336: and content attributes that conform to the following definition:
L7337: name
L7338: The value of the name attribute [html] after whitespace normalization [xml] MUST be viewport.
L7339: content
L7340: The value of the content attribute [html] after whitespace normalization [xml] MUST be of the following form:
L7341: (EBNF productions ISO/IEC
L7342: 14977)
L7343: All terminal symbols are in the Unicode Block 'Basic Latin' (U+0000
L7344: to U+007F).
L7345: viewport
L7346: =
L7347: property, { sep, property } ;
L7348: property
L7349: =
L7350: name, [ assign, value ] ;
L7351: name
L7352: =
L7353: ? character data ? ;
L7354: value
L7355: =
L7356: ? character data ? ;
L7357: sep
L7358: =
L7359: sep-char, { sep-char } ;
L7360: sep-char
L7361: =
L7362: ( ";" | "," | space ) ;
L7363: assign
L7364: =
L7365: [ space ], "=", [ space ] ;
L7366: space
L7367: =
L7368: #x20 ;
L7369: The only restriction on property names and values is that they MUST NOT contain separator characters or the assignment character.
L7370: The authoring requirements in this section apply after
L7371: whitespace normalization [xml] (i.e., after a reading
L7372: system strips leading and trailing whitespace and compacts all instances of multiple whitespace
L7373: within the attribute to single spaces). EPUB creators MAY include any whitespace characters [xml] in the authored tag so long as the result is valid to this
L7374: definition.
L7375: Note
L7376: Although [html] depends on the [infra] definition of whitespace, the Form Feed (U+000C)
L7377: character is not valid whitespace per the [xml] definition. It cannot be used in the XML
L7378: syntax (i.e., in XHTML content documents).
L7379: There are no restrictions on any other attributes allowed on the meta element by the [html] grammar.
L7380: Note
L7381: For more information about specifying the required height and width
L7382: properties, and their required values, refer to 8.2.2.6 Content document dimensions.
L7383: Although the viewport meta tag allows EPUB creators to use properties other than
L7384: height and width, and to not include values for these properties,
L7385: such use is strongly discouraged. Setting other properties may have unintended consequences on
L7386: the rendering of fixed-layout documents.
L7387: G. Schemas
L7388: This section is non-normative.
L7389: G.1 Package document schema
L7390: A schema for package documents is available at https://github.com/w3c/epubcheck/tree/master/src/main/resources/com/adobe/epubcheck/schema/30/package-30.nvdl.
L7391: Validation using this schema requires a processor that supports [nvdl], [relaxng-schema],
L7392: [isoschematron] and [xmlschema-2].
L7393: Note
L7394: The NVDL schema layer can be substituted by a multi-pass validation using the embedded RELAX NG
L7395: and ISO Schematron schemas alone.
L7396: Note
L7397: These schemas may be updated and corrected outside of formal revisions of this specification. As
L7398: a result, they are subject to change at any time.
L7399: G.2 OCF schemas
L7400: G.2.1 Schema for container.xml
L7401: A schema for container.xml files
L7402: is available at
L7403: https://github.com/w3c/epubcheck/tree/master/src/main/resources/com/adobe/epubcheck/schema/30/ocf-container-30.nvdl.
L7404: Validation using this schema requires a processor that supports [relaxng-schema] and
L7405: [xmlschema-2].
L7406: G.2.2 Schema for encryption.xml
L7407: The schema for encryption.xml
L7408: files is included in [xmlsec-rngschema-20130411].
L7409: G.2.3 Schema for signatures.xml
L7410: The schema for signatures.xml
L7411: files is included in [xmlsec-rngschema-20130411].
L7412: G.3 Media overlays schema
L7413: A schema for media overlay documents is available at https://github.com/w3c/epubcheck/tree/main/src/master/resources/com/adobe/epubcheck/schema/30/media-overlay-30.nvdl.
L7414: Validation using this schema requires a processor that supports [nvdl], [relaxng-schema],
L7415: [isoschematron] and [xmlschema-2].
L7416: Note
L7417: The NVDL schema layer can be substituted by a multi-pass validation using the embedded RELAX NG
L7418: and ISO Schematron schemas alone.
L7419: H. Detailed examples
L7420: This section is non-normative.
L7421: H.1 Resources
L7422: Consider the following extracts of a package document and an XHTML content document:
L7423: <package …>
L7424: <metadata …>
L7425: …
L7426: <link rel="record"
L7427: href="meta/data.xml"
L7428: media-type="application/marc"/>
L7429: <link rel="record"
L7430: href="https://www.example.org/meta/data2.xml"
L7431: media-type="application/marc"/>
L7432: …
L7433: </metadata>
L7434: <manifest>
L7435: …
L7436: <item id="page"
L7437: href="page.xhtml"
L7438: media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>
L7439: <item id="nav"
L7440: href="nav.xhtml"
L7441: media-type="application/xhtml+xml"
L7442: properties="nav"/>
L7443: <item id="style"
L7444: href="style.css"
L7445: media-type="text/css"/>
L7446: <item id="font_otf"
L7447: href="fonts/font-file.otf"
L7448: media-type="font/otf"/>
L7449: <item id="font_otf_remote"
L7450: href="https://www.example.org/fonts/font-file2.otf"
L7451: media-type="font/otf"/>
L7452: <item id="font_cff"
L7453: href="fonts/font-file.cff"
L7454: media-type="font/sfnt"/>
L7455: <item id="pls"
L7456: href="speech/cmn.pls"
L7457: media-type="application/pls+xml"/>
L7458: <item id="image_1"
L7459: href="media/image_1.png"
L7460: media-type="image/png"/>
L7461: <item id="image_2"
L7462: href="media/image_2.png"
L7463: media-type="image/png"
L7464: fallback="image_desc"/>
L7465: <item id="image_desc"
L7466: href="image_desc.xhtml"
L7467: media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>
L7468: <item id="image_3_heic"
L7469: href="media/image_3.heic"
L7470: media-type="image/heic"/>
L7471: <item id="image_3_png"
L7472: href="media/image_3.png"
L7473: media-type="image/png"/>
L7474: <item id="widget"
L7475: href="widget.xhtml"
L7476: media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>
L7477: …
L7478: </manifest>
L7479: <spine>
L7480: …
L7481: <itemref idref="page_001"/>
L7482: <itemref idref="image_2"/>
L7483: …
L7484: </spine>
L7485: </package>
L7486: <html …>
L7487: <head …>
L7488: …
L7489: <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="style.css"/>
L7490: <link rel="pronunciation" type="application/pls+xml" href="speech/cmn.pls"/>
L7491: …
L7492: </head>
L7493: <body>
L7494: <img src="media/image1_png"/>
L7495: …
L7496: <a href="media/image_2.png">…</a>
L7497: …
L7498: <picture>
L7499: <source srcset="media/image_3.heic" type="image/heic"/>
L7500: <img src="media/image_3.png"/>
L7501: </picture>
L7502: …
L7503: <iframe src="widget.xhtml"></iframe>
L7504: …
L7505: <a href="https://www.example.org/some_content">…</a>
L7506: </body>
L7507: </html>
L7508: The various resources in the EPUB publication can be categorized as follows. (Refer to 3. Publication resources for more information about these categories.)
L7509: meta/data.xml
L7510: The resource is a metadata record, stored in the EPUB container. It is linked via a
L7511: link element in the package document metadata. It is therefore a linked resource on
L7512: the manifest plane (i.e., is not listed in the manifest). It is not
L7513: part on any other planes.
L7514: https://www.example.org/meta/data2.xml
L7515: The resource is a metadata record, stored remotely. It is linked via a link element in
L7516: the package document metadata. It is therefore a linked resource on the manifest plane,
L7517: (i.e., it is not listed in the manifest). It is not part on any other planes.
L7518: page.xhtml
L7519: The resource is an XHTML document. It is listed in the [[EPUB spine | spine=]. It is a
L7520: publication resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, an EPUB content document on the spine plane, and is not present on the content plane. No fallback
L7521: is necessary.
L7522: nav.xhtml
L7523: The resource is the EPUB navigation document. It is not listed in the spine. It is a
L7524: publication resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, and is not present on
L7525: either the spine plane or the content plane. No fallback is necessary.
L7526: style.css
L7527: The resource is a CSS file. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced from an [html]
L7528: link element. It is a publication
L7529: resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, is not present on the spine plane, and
L7530: is a core media type resource on the content plane. No fallback is necessary.
L7531: font/font-file.otf
L7532: The resource is a TrueType font file. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced from a
L7533: CSS file. It is a publication resource on the manifest plane, is a container resource, is
L7534: not present on the spine plane, and is a core media type resource on the content plane. No
L7535: fallback is necessary.
L7536: https://www.example.org/fonts/font-file2.otf
L7537: The resource is a TrueType font file. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced from a
L7538: CSS file. It is a publication resource on the manifest plane, is a remote resource, is
L7539: not present on the spine plane, and is a core media type resource on the content plane. No
L7540: fallback is necessary.
L7541: font/font-file.cff
L7542: The resource is a font file in Compact Font Format. It is not listed in the spine but is
L7543: referenced from a CSS file. Its media type is not listed as a core media type. It is a publication resource on the
L7544: manifest plane, a container resource, is not present on the spine plane, and is an exempt resource on the content plane. No fallback is necessary.
L7545: speech/cmn.pls
L7546: The resource is a Pronunciation Lexicon file. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced
L7547: from an [html] link element. It is a
L7548: publication resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, not present on the spine
L7549: plane, and is an exempt resource on the content plane. No fallback is necessary.
L7550: image/image_1.png
L7551: The resource is a PNG image file. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced from an
L7552: [html] img element. It is a publication resource on the manifest plane, a container
L7553: resource, is not present on the spine plane, and is a core media type resource on the
L7554: content plane. No fallback is necessary.
L7555: image/image_2.png
L7556: The resource is a PNG image file. It is referenced via an [html] a element. Because it
L7557: is referenced from a hyperlink, it must be listed in the spine. It is a publication
L7558: resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, a foreign content document on the
L7559: spine plane, and a core media type resource on the content plane. As a foreign content
L7560: document, a fallback is required, which is provided via a manifest fallback.
L7561: image_desc.xhtml
L7562: The resource is an XHTML document. It is the "target" of a manifest fallback so is not
L7563: explicitly listed in the spine (but it "replaces" the existing spine item when needed). It
L7564: is a publication resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, an EPUB content
L7565: document on spine plane, and, because it is not "used" when rendering another EPUB content
L7566: document, it is not present on the content plane. No fallback is necessary.
L7567: image/image_3.heic
L7568: The resource is a High Efficiency (HEIC) image file. It is not listed in the spine but is
L7569: referenced from an [html] source element. Its media type is not listed as a core media type. It is a publication resource on the
L7570: manifest plane, a container resource, is not present on the spine plane, and is a foreign
L7571: resource on the content plane. As a foreign resource, a fallback is required, which is
L7572: provided via the sibling [html] img element in an [html] picture element.
L7573: image/image_3.png
L7574: The resource is a PNG image file. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced from an
L7575: [html] img element that is used as an intrinsic fallback of the [html] picture
L7576: element. It is a publication resource on the manifest plane, a container resource, is not
L7577: present on the spine plane, and is a core media type resource on the content plane. No
L7578: fallback is necessary.
L7579: widget.xhtml
L7580: The resource is an XHTML document. It is not listed in the spine but is referenced from an
L7581: [html] iframe element. It is a publication resource on the manifest plane, a container
L7582: resource, is not present on spine plane, and, because it is "used" when rendering another
L7583: EPUB content document, a core media type resource on the content plane. No fallback is
L7584: necessary.
L7585: https://www.example.org/some_content
L7586: The resource is referenced via an [html] a element and is not stored in the EPUB
L7587: container. Reading systems will normally open this link via a separate browser instance. It
L7588: is not on any planes defined by this specification.
L7589: Additional examples on the usage of different types of resources can be found in 5.6.2.2 Examples.
L7590: H.2 Scripting contexts
L7591: Consider the following example package document:
L7592: <package …>
L7593: …
L7594: <manifest>
L7595: …
L7596: <item id="chap01"
L7597: href="scripted01.xhtml"
L7598: media-type="application/xhtml+xml"
L7599: properties="scripted"/>
L7600: <item id="inset01"
L7601: href="scripted02.xhtml"
L7602: media-type="application/xhtml+xml"
L7603: properties="scripted"/>
L7604: <item id="slideshowjs"
L7605: href="slideshow.js"
L7606: media-type="text/javascript"/>
L7607: </manifest>
L7608: <spine …>
L7609: <itemref idref="chap01"/>
L7610: …
L7611: </spine>
L7612: …
L7613: </package>
L7614: and the following file scripted01.xhtml:
L7615: <html …>
L7616: <head>
L7617: …
L7618: <script type="text/javascript">
L7619: const te = navigator.epubReadingSystem.hasFeature("touch-events");
L7620: const te_message = te ? "passes" : "does not pass";
L7621: alert(`The reading system ${te_message} touch events to the content.`);
L7622: </script>
L7623: </head>
L7624: <body>
L7625: …
L7626: <iframe src="scripted02.xhtml" … />
L7627: …
L7628: </body>
L7629: </html>
L7630: and the following file scripted02.xhtml:
L7631: <html …>
L7632: <head>
L7633: …
L7634: <script type="text/javascript" href="slideshow.js"></script>
L7635: </head>
L7636: <body>
L7637: …
L7638: </body>
L7639: </html>
L7640: From these examples, it is true that:
L7641: the code in the script element in the head in
L7642: scripted01.xhtml is a spine-level script
L7643: because the document is referenced from the spine; and
L7644: the code in the script element in scripted02.xhtml is a container-constrained script because the
L7645: XHTML document it occurs in is included in scripted01.xhtml via the
L7646: iframe element.
L7647: H.3 Packaged EPUB
L7648: This example demonstrates the use of the OCF format to contain a signed and encrypted EPUB publication within an OCF ZIP container.
L7649: Ordered list of files in the OCF ZIP container:
L7650: mimetype
L7651: META-INF/container.xml
L7652: META-INF/signatures.xml
L7653: META-INF/encryption.xml
L7654: EPUB/As_You_Like_It.opf
L7655: EPUB/book.html
L7656: EPUB/nav.html
L7657: EPUB/images/cover.png
L7658: The contents of the mimetype file
L7659: application/epub+zip
L7660: The contents of the META-INF/container.xml file
L7661: <?xml version="1.0"?>
L7662: <container
L7663: version="1.0"
L7664: xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container">
L7665: <rootfiles>
L7666: <rootfile
L7667: full-path="EPUB/As_You_Like_It.opf"
L7668: media-type="application/oebps-package+xml"/>
L7669: </rootfiles>
L7670: </container>
L7671: The contents of the META-INF/signatures.xml file
L7672: <signatures
L7673: xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container">
L7674: <Signature
L7675: Id="AsYouLikeItSignature"
L7676: xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#">
L7677: <!--
L7678: SignedInfo is the information that is actually signed.
L7679: In this case, the SHA-1 algorithm is used to sign the
L7680: canonical form of the XML documents enumerated in the
L7681: Object element below.
L7682: -->
L7683: <SignedInfo>
L7684: <CanonicalizationMethod
L7685: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/TR/2001/REC-xml-c14n-20010315"/>
L7686: <SignatureMethod
L7687: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#dsa-sha1"/>
L7688: <Reference
L7689: URI="#AsYouLikeIt">
L7690: <DigestMethod
L7691: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#sha1"/>
L7692: <DigestValue>
L7693: …
L7694: </DigestValue>
L7695: </Reference>
L7696: </SignedInfo>
L7697: <!--
L7698: The signed value of the digest above, using the DSA
L7699: algorithm
L7700: -->
L7701: <SignatureValue>
L7702: …
L7703: </SignatureValue>
L7704: <!--
L7705: The key used to validate the signature
L7706: -->
L7707: <KeyInfo>
L7708: <KeyValue>
L7709: <DSAKeyValue>
L7710: <P>…</P>
L7711: <Q>…</Q>
L7712: <G>…</G>
L7713: <Y>…</Y>
L7714: </DSAKeyValue>
L7715: </KeyValue>
L7716: </KeyInfo>
L7717: <!--
L7718: The list of resources to sign (note that the canonical
L7719: form of XML documents is signed, while the binary form
L7720: of all other resources is used)
L7721: -->
L7722: <Object>
L7723: <Manifest
L7724: Id="AsYouLikeIt">
L7725: <Reference
L7726: URI="EPUB/As_You_Like_It.opf">
L7727: <Transforms>
L7728: <Transform
L7729: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/TR/2001/REC-xml-c14n-20010315"/>
L7730: </Transforms>
L7731: <DigestMethod
L7732: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#sha1"/>
L7733: <DigestValue>
L7734: </DigestValue>
L7735: </Reference>
L7736: <Reference URI="EPUB/book.html">
L7737: <Transforms>
L7738: <Transform
L7739: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/TR/2001/REC-xml-c14n-20010315"/>
L7740: </Transforms>
L7741: <DigestMethod
L7742: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#sha1"/>
L7743: <DigestValue>
L7744: </DigestValue>
L7745: </Reference>
L7746: <Reference
L7747: URI="EPUB/images/cover.png">
L7748: <DigestMethod
L7749: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#sha1"/>
L7750: <DigestValue>
L7751: </DigestValue>
L7752: </Reference>
L7753: </Manifest>
L7754: </Object>
L7755: </Signature>
L7756: </signatures>
L7757: The contents of the META-INF/encryption.xml file
L7758: <?xml version="1.0"?>
L7759: <encryption
L7760: xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container"
L7761: xmlns:enc="http://www.w3.org/2001/04/xmlenc#"
L7762: xmlns:ds="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#">
L7763: <!--
L7764: The RSA-encrypted AES-128 symmetric key used to encrypt
L7765: data enumerated in EncryptedData blocks below
L7766: -->
L7767: <enc:EncryptedKey
L7768: Id="EK">
L7769: <enc:EncryptionMethod
L7770: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2001/04/xmlenc#rsa-1_5"/>
L7771: <ds:KeyInfo>
L7772: <ds:KeyName>
L7773: John Smith
L7774: </ds:KeyName>
L7775: </ds:KeyInfo>
L7776: <enc:CipherData>
L7777: <enc:CipherValue>
L7778: xyzabc…
L7779: </enc:CipherValue>
L7780: </enc:CipherData>
L7781: </enc:EncryptedKey>
L7782: <!--
L7783: Each EncryptedData block identifies a single resource
L7784: that has been encrypted using the AES-128 algorithm.
L7785: The data remains stored, in its encrypted form, in the
L7786: original file within the container.
L7787: -->
L7788: <enc:EncryptedData Id="ED1">
L7789: <enc:EncryptionMethod
L7790: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2001/04/xmlenc#kw-aes128"/>
L7791: <ds:KeyInfo>
L7792: <ds:RetrievalMethod
L7793: URI="#EK"
L7794: Type="http://www.w3.org/2001/04/xmlenc#EncryptedKey"/>
L7795: </ds:KeyInfo>
L7796: <enc:CipherData>
L7797: <enc:CipherReference
L7798: URI="EPUB/book.html"/>
L7799: </enc:CipherData>
L7800: </enc:EncryptedData>
L7801: <enc:EncryptedData Id="ED2">
L7802: <enc:EncryptionMethod
L7803: Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2001/04/xmlenc#kw-aes128"/>
L7804: <ds:KeyInfo>
L7805: <ds:RetrievalMethod
L7806: URI="#EK" Type="http://www.w3.org/2001/04/xmlenc#EncryptedKey"/>
L7807: </ds:KeyInfo>
L7808: <enc:CipherData>
L7809: <enc:CipherReference
L7810: URI="EPUB/images/cover.png"/>
L7811: </enc:CipherData>
L7812: </enc:EncryptedData>
L7813: </encryption>

L7880: H.4 Clock values
L7881: The following are examples of allowed clock values:
L7882: 5:34:31.396 = 5 hours, 34 minutes, 31 seconds, and 396 milliseconds
L7883: 124:59:36 = 124 hours, 59 minutes, and 36 seconds
L7884: 0:05:01.2 = 5 minutes, 1 second, and 200 milliseconds
L7885: 0:00:04 = 4 seconds
L7886: 09:58 = 9 minutes and 58 seconds
L7887: 00:56.78 = 56 seconds and 780 milliseconds
L7888: 76.2s = 76.2 seconds = 76 seconds and 200 milliseconds
L7889: 7.75h = 7.75 hours = 7 hours and 45 minutes
L7890: 13min = 13 minutes
L7891: 2345ms = 2345 milliseconds
L7892: 12.345 = 12 seconds and 345 milliseconds
L7893: I. Media type registrations
L7894: I.1 The application/oebps-package+xml media type
L7895: This appendix registers the media type application/oebps-package+xml for the EPUB
L7896: package document. This registration supersedes RFC4839 (see https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc4839).
L7897: The package document is an XML file that describes an EPUB publication. It identifies the resources
L7898: in the EPUB publication and provides metadata information. The package document and its related
L7899: specifications are maintained and defined by the World Wide Web
L7900: Consortium (W3C).
L7901: MIME media type name:
L7902: application
L7903: MIME subtype name:
L7904: oebps-package+xml
L7905: Required parameters:
L7906: None.
L7907: Optional parameters:
L7908: None.
L7909: Encoding considerations:
L7910: 8bit if UTF-8; binary if UTF-16.
L7911: Package documents are in XML, represented either in UTF-8 or UTF-16. When the package
L7912: document is written in UTF-8, the file is 8bit compatible. When it is written in UTF-16, the
L7913: binary content-transfer-encoding must be used. For further details, see [rfc7303].
L7914: Security considerations:
L7915: Package documents contain well-formed XML conforming to the XML 1.0 specification.
L7916: Clearly, it is possible to author malicious files which, for example, contain malformed data.
L7917: Most XML parsers protect themselves from such attacks by rigorously enforcing
L7918: conformance.
L7919: All processors that read package documents should rigorously check the size and validity of
L7920: data retrieved.
L7921: There is no current provision in the EPUB 3 specification for encryption, signing, or
L7922: authentication within the package document format.
L7923: Interoperability considerations:
L7924: None.
L7925: Published specification:
L7926: This media type registration is for the EPUB package document, as described by the EPUB 3
L7927: specification located at https://www.w3.org/TR/epub-33/.
L7928: The EPUB 3 specification supersedes the Open Packaging Format 2.0.1 specification, which is
L7929: located at https://idpf.org/epub/20/spec/OPF_2.0.1_draft.htm and which also uses the application/oepbs-package+xml media type.
L7930: Applications which use this media type:
L7931: This media type is in wide use for the distribution of ebooks in the EPUB format.
L7932: Additional information:
L7933: Magic number(s):
L7934: none
L7935: File extension(s):
L7936: .opf
L7937: Macintosh File Type Code(s):
L7938: TEXT
L7939: Fragment identifiers:
L7940: EPUB Canonical Fragment Identifiers are custom fragment identifiers defined for EPUB Publications. They may be used to refer to an arbitrary content within any
L7941: publication resource defined for the publication. These identifiers are defined
L7942: at https://idpf.org/epub/linking/cfi/.
L7943: Person & email address to contact for further information:
L7944: EPUB 3 Working Group (public-epub-wg@w3.org)
L7945: Intended usage:
L7946: COMMON
L7947: Author/Change controller:
L7948: World Wide Web Consortium (W3C)
L7949: I.2 The application/epub+zip media type
L7950: This appendix registers the media type application/epub+zip for the EPUB Open Container
L7951: Format (OCF).
L7952: An OCF ZIP container, or EPUB container, file is a container technology based on the zip archive
L7953: format (see https://pkware.cachefly.net/webdocs/casestudies/APPNOTE.TXT). It is used to encapsulate the
L7954: EPUB publication. OCF and its related standards are maintained and defined by the World Wide Web Consortium (W3C).
L7955: MIME media type name:
L7956: application
L7957: MIME subtype name:
L7958: epub+zip
L7959: Required parameters:
L7960: None.
L7961: Optional parameters:
L7962: None.
L7963: Encoding considerations:
L7964: OCF ZIP container files are binary files encoded in the
L7965: application/zip media type.
L7966: Security considerations:
L7967: All processors that read OCF ZIP container files should rigorously check the size and
L7968: validity of data retrieved.
L7969: In addition, because of the various content types that can be embedded in OCF ZIP container
L7970: files, application/epub+zip may describe content that poses security
L7971: implications beyond those noted here. However, only in cases where the processor recognizes
L7972: and processes the additional content, or where further processing of that content is
L7973: dispatched to other processors, would security issues potentially arise. In such cases,
L7974: matters of security would fall outside the domain of this registration document.
L7975: Security considerations that apply to application/zip also apply to OCF ZIP
L7976: container files.
L7977: Interoperability considerations:
L7978: None.
L7979: Published specification:
L7980: This media type registration is for the EPUB Open Container Format (OCF), as described by the
L7981: EPUB 3 specification located at https://www.w3.org/TR/epub-33/.
L7982: The EPUB 3 specification supersedes both RFC 4839 and the Open Container
L7983: Format 2.0.1 specification, which is located at https://idpf.org/epub/20/spec/OCF_2.0.1_draft.doc, and which also
L7984: uses the application/epub+zip media type.
L7985: Applications that use this media type:
L7986: This media type is in wide use for the distribution of ebooks in the EPUB format.
L7987: Additional information:
L7988: Magic number(s):
L7989: 0: PK 0x03 0x04, 30: mimetype, 38:
L7990: application/epub+zip
L7991: File extension(s):
L7992: OCF ZIP container files are most often identified with the extension
L7993: .epub.
L7994: Macintosh file type code(s):
L7995: ZIP
L7996: Fragment identifiers:
L7997: EPUB Canonical Fragment Identifiers are custom fragment identifiers defined for EPUB Publications. They may be used to refer to an arbitrary content within any
L7998: publication resource defined for the publication. These identifiers are defined
L7999: at https://idpf.org/epub/linking/cfi/.
L8000: Person & email address to contact for further information:
L8001: EPUB 3 Working Group (public-epub-wg@w3.org)
L8002: Intended usage:
L8003: COMMON
L8004: Author/change controller:
L8005: World Wide Web Consortium (W3C)
L8006: J. Index
L8007: J.1 Terms defined by this specification
L8008: audio

L8350: K. Change log
L8351: This section is non-normative.
L8352: Note that this change log only identifies substantive changes since EPUB 3.2 — those that may affect the conformance of
L8353: EPUB publications.
L8354: For a list of all issues addressed, refer to the Working Group's issue tracker.
L8355: Substantive changes since the Recommendation of 2023-05-25
L8356: 16-July-2024: Fixed the definition of whitespace allowed in viewport meta tags to refer to the
L8357: characters allowed by the XML standard. See issue 2637.
L8358: 25-July-2023: Updated where the epub:type is allowed on SVGs to use the definition
L8359: of a renderable element. See issue
L8360: 2556.
L8361: 25-July-2023: Clarified the definitions of embedded SVGs and moved the restriction on where the
L8362: epub:type attribute is allowed to apply so it applies to all SVG definitions.
L8363: See issue 2555.
L8364: Substantive changes since the Candidate Recommendation of 2023-02-21
L8365: 17-March-2023: replaced the deprecated JavaScript attribute name by
L8366: hasFeature in an example. See issue 2543.
L8367: Substantive changes since the Candidate Recommendation of 2022-05-12
L8368: 11-Jan-2023: Marked the dir attribute under-implemented due to lack of support in
L8369: reading systems. See pull request
L8370: 2515.
L8371: 09-Jan-2023: Fixed incorrect OPUS media type. See issue 2516.
L8372: 05-Jan-2023: Removed the precedence rules for linked records as reading systems do not support
L8373: their processing. See pull request
L8374: 2512.
L8375: 16-Dec-2022: Clarified that the special files for processing the OCF container are not listed in
L8376: the manifest, so the restriction that the manifest only list publication resources is not
L8377: needed. See pull request 2506.
L8378: 12-Dec-2022: Clarified that fixed layout height and width declarations must be in the first
L8379: viewport meta tag. See pull request 2503.
L8380: 08-Dec-2022: Removed the acquire and xmp properties after finding no
L8381: evidence these are used by publishers or reading systems. See issue 2489.
L8382: 29-Nov-2022: Clarified that data URLs are not unique publication resources, and not allowed in
L8383: the package document, but are subject to fallback requirements. See issue 2485.
L8384: 29-Nov-2022: Clarified the epub:type attribute is not allowed on the
L8385: head element and metadata content in XHTML content documents. See issue 2486.
L8386: 17-Nov-2022: Updated file paths and names to identify that they are scalar value strings in the
L8387: OCF abstract container, not just case sensitive. See issue 2461.
L8388: 11-Nov-2022: Added caution about reading systems not retaining HTML element-based rendering
L8389: instructions in navigation document elements. See issue 2477.
L8390: 02-Nov-2022: Removed the restriction on deprecated characters in the Tags and Variation
L8391: Selectors Supplement and replaced with a general note to avoid the use of characters already
L8392: deprecated by the Unicode standard, as the list changes over time. See issue 2469.
L8393: 20-Oct-2022: Removed requirement to encode file names in abstract container in UTF-8 as it is
L8394: not possible in the abstract and is already covered by a ZIP container requirement. See issue 2461.
L8395: 17-Oct-2022: Added recommendation against using spaces in file paths and names. See issue 2458.
L8396: 15-Oct-2022: Allow properties without values in viewport meta tag definition. See
L8397: pull request 2457.
L8398: 11-Oct-2022: Added additional requirement that viewport meta height and width not
L8399: be declared multiple times. See issue
L8400: 2442.
L8401: 21-Sept-2022: Made the requirement to declare property prefixes explicit. See issue 2438.
L8402: 19-Sept-2022: Removed minor contradictions in the epub:type attribute usage
L8403: definitions. See issue 2434.
L8404: 14-Sept-2022: Combined the legacy feature section into the package document definition. See issue 2423.
L8405: 14-Sept-2022: Clarified that the href attribute in the package document must not be
L8406: used to reference other package document elements (i.e., to indirectly reference a resource via
L8407: its manifest or spine entry). See issue
L8408: 2420.
L8409: 14-Sept-2022: Re-establish that empty reference and property values are not valid to ensure that
L8410: metadata properties do not consist only of prefixes. See issue 2417.
L8411: 14-Sept-2022: Added a new section for expressing all the primary navigation document content
L8412: requirements. See issue 2421.
L8413: 7-Sept-2022: Added clarifying requirement that the manifest only list publication resources. See
L8414: issue 2413.
L8415: 28-Aug-2022: Added a note to Media Overlays text element definition regarding using
L8416: embedded timed media. See issue
L8417: 2397.
L8418: 02-Aug-2022: Updated the media type registrations, following the official IANA
L8419: review comments on updating the previous registrations. See issue 1398.
L8420: 28-July-2022: The restrictions on the elements where epub:type may be used has been
L8421: made explicit. See issue 2377.
L8422: 18-July-2022: The viewport meta element for specifying the initial containing
L8423: boundary in fixed-layout documents is now formally defined. See issue 2292.
L8424: 14-July-2022: Clarified that the markup in the SVG title element is restricted to
L8425: HTML elements. See issue 2355.
L8426: 10-June-2022: Clarified that data blocks are exempt from fallback requirements. See issue 2331.
L8427: 07-June-2022: The usage of File URLs has been disallowed. See issue 2324.
L8428: 07-June-2022: Media type registration sections are now normative. See issue 2313.
L8429: 07-June-2022: Updated privacy and security recommendations to use normative language. See pull request 2297.
L8430: 27-May-2022: Added recommendation to only reference remote resources via https. See issue 2263.
L8431: 20-May-2022: Add recommendation not to store sensitive user data in persistent storage, and to
L8432: encrypt it if there is no other choice. See issue 2264.
L8433: 17-May-2022: Added an index of terms. See issue 2260.
L8434: Substantive changes since EPUB
L8435: 3.2
L8436: 12-Apr-2022: Added note about complexities of escaping from nested escapable structures and
L8437: updated the list of semantics to use for escaping to match the guidance. See issue 2221.
L8438: 6-Apr-2022: Removed the restrictions on the value of the authority property and
L8439: added a note referencing the old IDPF registry. See issue 2200.
L8440: 31-Mar-2022: Removed the restriction on deprecated MathML features and added a general caution
L8441: that any technology may make changes that can cause an EPUB publication to become invalid. See
L8442: issue 2118.
L8443: 31-Mar-2022: Reformulated custom attributes as a content authoring feature and added a new
L8444: section on custom rendering properties. See issue 2134.
L8445: 25-Mar-2022: Fixed conflicting statements about the requirement for semantics in media overlay
L8446: documents and clarified requirements for skippability and escapability. See issue 2066.
L8447: 22-Mar-2022: Removed the recommendation that reading systems recognize the built-in
L8448: collection-type values and replaced with a note about enabling improved handling of related
L8449: content. See issue 2071.
L8450: 21-Mar-2022: Add a tolerance of one second for the sum of the individual media overlay docuemnts
L8451: matching the total duration. See issue
L8452: 2093.
L8453: 17-Mar-2022: Deprecate the creation of new collection types. See issue 2060.
L8454: 17-Mar-2022: Removed dated requirements on the use of epub:type that suggest
L8455: equivalence with ARIA roles. See issue
L8456: 2070.
L8457: 16-Mar-2022: Add new section on conformance checking and definition for EPUB conformance
L8458: checker. See pull request 2025.
L8459: 14-Mar-2022: Renamed the term "valid-relative-container-URL-with-fragment" to
L8460: "valid-relative-ocf-URL-with-fragment string". See issue 2076.
L8461: 09-Mar-2022: Restore requirement that valid-relative-container-URL-with-fragment strings resolve
L8462: to resources in the OCF abstract container. See issue 2024.
L8463: 09-Mar-2022: Added NCX doctype to allowed external identifiers. See issue 2045.
L8464: 08-Mar-2022: Require use of the fixed layout property values defined in this specification. See
L8465: issue 2039.
L8466: 08-Mar-2022: Clarified that the refines attribute must not be used with global fixed layout
L8467: property declarations. See issue
L8468: 2036.
L8469: 07-Mar-2022: Consolidated the usage requirements for manifest properties into a new Resource
L8470: Properties section. See issue
L8471: 2030.
L8472: 05-Mar-2022: Forbid circular references and self-references in refinement chains. See issue 2031.
L8473: 19-Feb-2022: Clarified the audio element's definition by making it optional and adapted the
L8474: specification's text elsewhere to address the situation when the element is indeed not present.
L8475: See issue 1986.
L8476: 04-Feb-2022: Expanded the section on security and privacy to include new sections on the threat
L8477: model for EPUB publications and additional recommendations for ensuring security and privacy.
L8478: See issue 1871, issue 1872, issue 1875 and issue 1876.
L8479: 21-Jan-2022: term "risky", used for the class of unsupported features, has been renamed to
L8480: "under-implemented". See the WG resolution.
L8481: 08-Dec-2021: The page-spread-center property is now an alias for
L8482: spread-none. See issue
L8483: 1929.
L8484: 03-Dec-2021: Added a new risky class for unsupported features. See issue 1944.
L8485: 03-Dec-2021: Remove the element-based restrictions on remote resources. See issue 1913.
L8486: 01-Dec-2021: Deprecated the use of page-spread-center. It is now an alias for
L8487: spread-none. See issue
L8488: 1929.
L8489: 26-Nov-2021: A requirement and an algorithm to detect out-of-container URLs has been added to
L8490: the specification. See issue
L8491: 1912.
L8492: 18-Nov-2021: Change to only disallow deprecated characters in the Tags and Variation Selectors
L8493: Supplement. See issue 1885.
L8494: 12-Nov-2021: Change the recommendation to use SHA-1 to encrypt the obfuscation key to a
L8495: requirement. See issue 1873.
L8496: 12-Nov-2021: Restrict the obfuscation algorithm to fonts and add caution to use better
L8497: protection methods whenever possible. See issue 1873.
L8498: 12-Nov-2021: Removed the statement about rights.xml being reserved for future standardization of
L8499: DRM information. See issue 1874.
L8500: 10-Nov-2021: Proper definition of the content URL and handling of relative URLs. See issue 1374 and issue 1888
L8501: 29-Oct-2021: Recommended that EPUB creators not use path-absolute-URL strings for referencing
L8502: resources due to the lack of a consistent root. See issue 1681.
L8503: 18-Oct-2021: Clarified the contexts from which remote resources may be referenced. See issue 1857.
L8504: 12-Oct-2021: The Structure Semantics Vocabulary has been moved into a separate Working Group
L8505: Note. See issue 1763.
L8506: 27-Aug-2021: Add clarifications for including landmarks, such as limiting the number,
L8507: recommending known semantics, and ensuring the labels are human readable. Also added
L8508: recommendation to not include nested lists to match the page list. See issue 1761.
L8509: 22-July-2021: Clarified TTS handling of images in media overlays. See issue 1745.
L8510: 09-July-2021: Restored the custom attributes section due to known usage but without reference to
L8511: the attribute registry. See issue
L8512: 1602.
L8513: 09-July-2021: Added the "Relationship to URL" section to explain the use of URL standard
L8514: terminology in this document relative to resource formats that do not reference it. See issue 1726.
L8515: 05-July-2021: Removed the section on private use area characters from the XHTML restrictions.
L8516: The issues are more complex than what is covered and not in scope of EPUB to define. See issue 1732.
L8517: 28-June-2021: Added a note discouraging EPUB creators from referencing resources outside the
L8518: directory containing the package document to avoid interoperability issues. See issue 1687
L8519: 23-June-2021: Added the base element to the list of discouraged XHTML constructs.
L8520: See issue 1699.
L8521: 18-June-2021: Moved requirements for authoring SSML, PLS lexicons and CSS 3 Speech to the EPUB 3 Text-to-Speech Enhancements note. The
L8522: ability to use these technologies in EPUB 3 publications remains unchanged. See issue 1690.
L8523: 16-June-2021: Absolute URLs with file scheme should not be used on manifest items.
L8524: See issue 1688.
L8525: 31-May-2021: Require Unicode normalization and full case folding (in this order) for file name
L8526: uniqueness comparisons. See issue
L8527: 1631 and pull request
L8528: 1648.
L8529: 31-May-2021: Confirmed that SVG content documents do not have to be valid to the SVG
L8530: specification, only meet the well-formedness and ID requirements currently referenced and the
L8531: restrictions imposed by this specification. See issue 1323.
L8532: 12-May-2021: Clarified that manifest items must not contain fragment identifiers. See issue 1303.
L8533: 12-May-2021: Changed all references to URIs/IRIs to URLs and references to RFCs 3986 and 3987 to
L8534: the URL standard. See issue 808.
L8535: 08-May-2021: Added clarifying text that link element can be used to link individual
L8536: metadata properties in an alternative format. See issue 1666.
L8537: 06-May-2021: Noted that the working group will no longer maintain the publication type and
L8538: collection role registries and removed dependence on the latter for validating collection types
L8539: (use of NMToken values remains restricted to extension specifications). The registry of
L8540: authority codes is now integrated into the property definition. See pull request 1664.
L8541: 06-May-2021: Added application/ecmascript as a core media type for scripts. See issue 1353.
L8542: 06-May-2021: Added new section on fragment identifiers to media overlays and now recommend HTML
L8543: target element references and SVG fragment identifiers instead of requiring conformance to the
L8544: incompatible XPointer Shorthand syntax. See issue 1586.
L8545: 28-Apr-2021: Drop requirement for resources referenced from HTML link elements to
L8546: have core media type fallbacks. See issue 1312.
L8547: 22-Apr-2021: The usage of UTF-16 for CSS and XML has been changed, UTF-8 is the recommended
L8548: encoding. See issue 1628.
L8549: 19-Apr-2021: The use of custom attributes in EPUB content documents is no longer supported. See
L8550: issue 1602.
L8551: 13-Apr-2021: Require path names in OCF to also be UTF-8 encoded. See issue 1630.
L8552: 12-Apr-2021: Added a reference to the SVG direction attribute in 6.3.1.2 CSS requirements. See issue
L8553: 1614.
L8554: 09-Apr-2021: Added a new section dedicated to accessibility in EPUB publications.
L8555: 04-May-2021: Removed requirements around SVG requiredExtensions attribute. See issue 1087.
L8556: 26-Mar-2021: Removed requirement for page list ordering to reflect the order of page breaks in
L8557: the content. See issue 1500.
L8558: 26-Mar-2021: Allowed dc:creator and dc:contributor elements to have
L8559: multiple roles and allowed roles for publisher. See issue 1129 and issue 1583
L8560: 23-Mar-2021: Clarified the requirements for the use of data URLs in EPUB publications. See issue 1564.
L8561: 17-Mar-2021: Include non characters at the end of the supplementary planes in list of characters
L8562: not allowed in file names. See issue
L8563: 1538.
L8564: 15-Mar-2021: Removed the normative dependencies on XML schemas and added element and attribute
L8565: definitions for the container.xml, encryption.xml and
L8566: signatures.xml files. All schemas are considered non-normative. See issue 1566.
L8567: 10-Mar-2021: Require that resources referenced from an EPUB publication not be located in the
L8568: META-INF directory. See issue 1205.
L8569: 08-Mar-2021: The fix for issue 1322
L8570: on 20-Jan-2021 incorrectly mentioned EPUB content documents having durations. Corrected to media
L8571: overlay documents.
L8572: 08-Mar-2021: Added recommendation that refines attribute use fragment identifiers
L8573: to reference publication resources. See issue 1361.
L8574: 08-Mar-2021: Change requirement that media overlay document par and
L8575: seq ordering match the default reading order to guidance. See issue 1458
L8576: 05-Mar-2021: Clarified that whitespace within metadata element values is collapsed per the
L8577: [infra] specification definition. See issue 1528.
L8578: 26-Feb-2021: Created a new section for describing general metadata value requirements,
L8579: specifically whitespace handling. See issue 1528.
L8580: 17-Feb-2020: File extension recommendations have been removed (affects the package document,
L8581: XHTML content documents, media overlay documents). See issue 1294.
L8582: 15-Feb-2021: Clarified that nav elements without an epub:type
L8583: attribute are not subject to the EPUB navigation document's content model restrictions. See issue 976.
L8584: 10-Feb-2021: A first draft of the security and privacy
L8585: section has been added.
L8586: 04-Feb-2021: Clarify that the value of dc:language elements must be well-formed
L8587: language tags. See issue 1325.
L8588: 02-Feb-2021: Added auto value for dir attribute and clarified the
L8589: precedence of the attribute. See issue
L8590: 1491 and issue 1494.
L8591: 02-Feb-2021: Added the hreflang attribute to link elements to identify
L8592: the language of linked resources. See issue 1488.
L8593: 20-Jan-2021: Clarified that user-defined media overlay style classes must be declared in the
L8594: package document metadata. See issue
L8595: 1319.
L8596: 20-Jan-2021: Add recommendation that the sum of the media overlay durations for each EPUB
L8597: content document match the total duration specified for the EPUB publication. See issue 1322.
L8598: 20-Jan-2021: Clarified that the epub:type attribute does not improve the
L8599: accessibility of publications. Added pointers to the role attribute and the
L8600: DPUB-ARIA vocabulary for accessibility.
L8601: 13-Jan-2021: The requirement for progressive enhancement with spine-level scripting has been
L8602: changed to a recommendation that top-level content documents remain consumable when scripting is
L8603: not available. See issue 1444.
L8604: 24-Dec-2020: The specification no longer refers to a release identifier, but the requirement to
L8605: include a last modification date remains for backwards compatibility. See issue 1440.
L8606: 16-Dec-2020: Terminology and requirements related to "renditions" of an EPUB publication have
L8607: been simplified to improve the readability of the specifications (i.e., to align with the
L8608: generally understood concept that an EPUB publication has only a single rendering described by a
L8609: single package document). These changes do not affect the ability to include multiple
L8610: renditions, which are now more fully covered in [epub-multi-rend-11]. See issue 1436.
L8611: 14-Nov-2020: The term "semantic inflection" is no longer used to describe the process of adding
L8612: structural semantics to elements. The term is not widely understood outside of EPUB and is
L8613: unnecessarily complex. The specification now simply refers to "expressing" or "adding"
L8614: structural semantics.
L8615: 09-Nov-2020: The requirement that the ordering of the toc nav match the ordering of
L8616: EPUB content documents in the spine, and the elements within each file, has been reduced to a
L8617: recommendation. See issue 1283.
L8618: 06-Nov-2020: Clarified that HTML script elements that contain data blocks are not instances of scripting. See issue 1352.
L8619: 06-Nov-2020: Added WebP to the image core media types. See issue 1344.
L8620: 06-Nov-2020: A restricted set of external identifiers are
L8621: now allowed in publication resources. References to external
L8622: entities from the internal DTD subset remain restricted, however. See issue 1338.
L8623: 12-Oct-2020: Added OPUS to the audio core media types with a
L8624: warning that it is still subject to review depending on support in Mac/iOS. See issue 645.
L8625: 30-Sept-2020: The structure of the EPUB core specifications has been simplified to ease
L8626: understanding and access to information. This specification now consolidates the authoring
L8627: requirements from the EPUB 3.2 specification together with the Packages, Content Documents, OCF
L8628: and Media Overlays specifications. A separate document, EPUB Reading Systems 3.3, consolidates
L8629: the reading system requirements from those documents.
L8630: L. Acknowledgements
L8631: This section is non-normative.
L8632: Specifications, like art, are human creations. No human has done more for EPUB than Garth Conboy, who has
L8633: been there every step of the way, from the very first OEB 1.0 in 1999 to today's EPUB 3.3. None of this
L8634: would have happened without Garth's vision, knowledge, and preternatural good nature. We dedicate EPUB 3.3 to his memory.
L8635: We are forever in your debt, Garth.
